Over the past two weeks, I've received several phone calls and have had follow -up discussions with several downtown and DDA businesses who are extremely upset with negative comments about Tenpenny Furniture That were made by a City Council person During the downtown discussion at the February 26th City Council meeting. In fact, Tenpenny Furniture Is an outstanding local business. They sell high -quality, brand name, Skilled craftsmanship furniture and accessories. Most of their products are American -made, And they carry Amish -designed and manufactured items as well. They are widely known for their excellent customer service. They have served this community for over 31 years. They are the largest downtown retailer. During most of their 31 years in business, They were the single largest business taxpayer in downtown up until recently. They also attract a wide spectrum of customers in a broad demographic range from communities throughout Oakland, Wayne, Washtenaw, Genesee, And livingston counties and beyond that is a huge plus for downtown South Lyon I hope that they will Continue to serve this community for many more years to come any downtown would be proud to have Them I would hope that over the next few months perhaps every other City Council meeting we could Have a relatively brief discussion on each of the four points of the main street downtown management philosophy. Those are, to refresh, organization, which is the role of the DDA with city support and partnership, the DDA's budget committees with citizen and business involvement, goals And objectives, proactive leadership, ethics, and communication. The second point, promotions And marketing, which is market information, local events and activities, wayfinding, merchandising, Media and publicity education communication and cultural programs the third point of Main Street is design recognizing our historic buildings and Historic preservation reuse of historic buildings infill architecture master Plan zoning ordinances infrastructure parking maintenance signage and the Natural environment and the fourth point economic vitality that again is market Information with market potential, business recruitment and retention, Business development, existing building data, having a handle on spaces available, Promoting that, business and property ownership relationships, financial Incentives and programs available, developer selection and developer Partnerships, broad retail service and housing mix for downtown, healthy Competition and collecting data to benchmark and compare success over time In closing all of those four points must work together to enhance downtown and to Preserve our sense of place for the future 2018 will be a very good year for downtown South Lyon a potentially great year if we all work together thank you For the opportunity to discuss these important issues I await your response Again, a copy is with the city clerk.
